  > Apache HertzBeat 1.9.1 is a maintenance release with collection, monitor management, credential and status-history fixes.

  > Management UI and API access now requires the exact `admin` role. Existing non-admin accounts are not automatically promoted. Review accounts and automation credentials before upgrading. Back up the database and deployment configuration, preserve `common.secret`, and use matching 1.9.1 manager and collector artifacts. ### Coddy — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/coddy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/coddy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-18) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/coddy)

> Thin wrapper that copies upstream's static Go binary out of its published scratch image onto Alpine (scratch has no shell for an entrypoint). The pin is the image tag = git tag <version>, no v prefix. MIT. See guide §5.27.

### Cognee AI Memory Platform with MCP — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/cognee_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/cognee_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-17) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/cognee-ai-memory-p-1)

> Standard storage is PostgreSQL/pgvector, not Ladybug; API raw files have no volume by default. Upgrade API/MCP together and retain a logical database backup plus raw archive for rollback; old images cannot read the newer Alembic revision. MCP backend credentials do not authenticate its public transport. API-mode string inputs are literal uploads; source-ingestion opt-ins belong on the API and remain unset. See guide §5.5.

### DSH + LongMemory — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/dsh_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/dsh_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-18) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/dsh-longmemory)

> Upstream ships prereleases only (alpha/rc every few days); the pin is an npm version whose git tag is dsh-v<version>. LongMemory is pinned in its own template repo. Sign-in is a generated DSH_GATE_PASSWORD, not the launch token: DSH mints that token with randomBytes() per process and it cannot be preset, so the gateway redeems it behind the edge gate and the deployer only sees a browser password prompt. The gate and auto sign-in are interlocked — clearing the password disables both. The gate and the redirect must live in a Caddy `route`, not a `handle`, or `redir` sorts ahead of `basic_auth` and an anonymous GET / is answered 302 with the live token; test-gateway.sh pins that case, and its stand-in must answer the exchange with the same Location as the pinned DSH (0.1.7 sends ./ where 0.1.5 sent /; the Caddyfile loop guard matches both). See guide §5.22.

- [v2026.11.11](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.11) 2026-11-20
  > `mise bootstrap` can now manage MacPorts ports and update mise itself before tools. GitHub, GitLab and Forgejo tools can follow the most recently published release, and Homebrew tap formulae can pour bottles and download from private GitHub releases. This release also fixes `http:` checksum verification, shims for `path:` versions, `mise unuse -g` with split global configs, Windows Server Core startup, and several task, bootstrap and Java issues.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > ### Bootstrap
  > 
  > - **MacPorts package manager.** On macOS, you can declare ports in `[bootstrap.packages]` with the `macports:` prefix and i …
- [v2026.11.10](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.10) 2026-11-18
  > `mise lock` gains an offline `--check` mode for CI, and `--dry-run` now shows what would actually change. New options let you limit mise to an allowlist of backends, choose musl or glibc builds for a single tool, pin PyPI packages to private uv indexes, and exclude git-ignored files from task `sources`. This release also fixes problems with lockfiles, Java build ordering, `mise exec` PATH order, Homebrew formulas, and shims.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > ### Lockfiles
  >

  > 
  > - Retry model discovery instead of caching total failures.
  > - Preserve successful partial catalogs, including empty vendor results.
  > - Allow manual Codex model selection when model listing fails.
  > - Localize errors when all vendors fail model discovery.
  > - Add regression tests for retries and Codex fallback.

### Marmot — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/marmot_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/marmot_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-18) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/marmot)

> Thin upstream image with loopback-only replacement of admin:admin before exposure. Only exact HTTP 401 Invalid credentials skips replacement; transport/status/JSON errors abort. Private files keep bootstrap secrets out of argv and owner-changed passwords survive. MRN upsert/resolve and assets:view/manage roles were tested on a fresh stack; no old-data migration. The 71 cached plugins measured about 1.7 GiB; signed-index security updates remain enabled. Cache is on the 5120 MB volume and refreshed when the Marmot release changes. Stable image tags omit v; git tags include it. Encryption key remains base64(sha256(MARMOT_ENCRYPTION_SEED)). MIT. See guide §5.34.

Newer releases (6):
- [v1.0-beta.52](https://github.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-server/releases/tag/v1.0-beta.52) 2026-11-08 (pre-release)
  > **Note: This is a beta version and NOT ready for use in production.**
  > 
  > ### Sync server
  > 
  > 1. Downloads of stored attachments are always served as a binary file to save (`application/octet-stream`, `Content-Disposition: attachment`), so a stored file can never open as a web page on the storage domain.
  > 2. Uploads, both direct and multipart, are always stored as `application/octet-stream`, whatever their name.
  > 3. New uploads must be named by the client's hash: letters and digits only, 1 to 64 characters. Names with an extension, such as `page.html`, are refused. Existing attachments keep the older …
- [v1.0-beta.51](https://github.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-server/releases/tag/v1.0-beta.51) 2026-11-08 (pre-release)
  > **Note: This is a beta version and NOT ready for use in production.**
  > 
  > ### Identity
  > 
  > 1. Email addresses are checked more strictly at signup and when changing email. Addresses are normalized (Unicode compatibility forms, internationalized domains), malformed or mixed-script look-alike addresses are rejected, and look-alike forms of the same address now map to one account.
  > 2. Deployments can plug in their own address checks through `IEmailRiskCheck`, enabled by setting `EMAIL_VALIDATOR_URL` and `EMAIL_VALIDATOR_SECRET`. Self-hosted servers without them behave as before. If the check is unreachab …
- [v1.0-beta.50](https://github.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-server/releases/tag/v1.0-beta.50) 2026-11-07 (pre-release)
  > **Note: This is a beta version and NOT ready for use in production.**
  > 
  > ### Sync
  > 
  > 1. A Redis outage no longer fails sync requests. The token introspection cache now falls back to introspecting tokens while Redis is unreachable, instead of failing every authenticated request. Revoking a session (logging out, changing the password) still fails until Redis is back, so a revoked token can't stay cached and keep working.
  > 2. Pushing no longer waits on Redis. Telling your other devices that a push completed now happens in the background, so a slow or unavailable Redis can't delay or fail the push itse …
- [v1.0-beta.49](https://github.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-server/releases/tag/v1.0-beta.49) 2026-11-07 (pre-release)
  > **Note: This is a beta version and NOT ready for use in production.**
  > 
  > ### Identity
  > 
  > 1. Password reset and email confirmation links no longer stop working when the identity server restarts or is redeployed, and they now work across multiple identity server instances. The keys that sign them are now kept in MongoDB (the new `dataprotection.keys` collection), encrypted with `NOTESNOOK_API_SECRET` like the token keys.

- [v1.0-beta.47](https://github.com/streetwriters/notesnook-sync-server/releases/tag/v1.0-beta.47) 2026-10-31 (pre-release)
  > **Note: This is a beta version and NOT ready for use in production.**
  > 
  > ### Sync
  > 
  > 1. Use less memory to receive items: pushed ciphers stay UTF-8 from the hub to MongoDB, in pooled buffers, instead of becoming strings that are encoded again for the database. Stored items are unchanged.
  > 2. Allocate less on every hub call: the account check uses a projection built once, and the hub no longer builds its collection lookups for each invocation
  > 3. Reject a cipher that is only base64 padding as corrupted
  >

  > ### Patch Changes
  > 
  > - On Windows, opening a folder in Cursor from Open Knowledge now opens that folder.
  > - Embedded pages in a document can no longer open other apps.
  > - On Windows, arguments Open Knowledge passes to a launcher script, such as an agent's or Cursor's, now arrive as written. A launch whose argument cannot be passed safely stops with an error that says so.

  > `mise bootstrap` can now manage MacPorts ports and update mise itself before tools. GitHub, GitLab and Forgejo tools can follow the most recently published release, and Homebrew tap formulae can pour bottles and download from private GitHub releases. This release also fixes `http:` checksum verification, shims for `path:` versions, `mise unuse -g` with split global configs, Windows Server Core startup, and several task, bootstrap and Java issues.

  > `mise lock` gains an offline `--check` mode for CI, and `--dry-run` now shows what would actually change. New options let you limit mise to an allowlist of backends, choose musl or glibc builds for a single tool, pin PyPI packages to private uv indexes, and exclude git-ignored files from task `sources`. This release also fixes problems with lockfiles, Java build ordering, `mise exec` PATH order, Homebrew formulas, and shims.

### ReMe — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/reme_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/reme_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-18) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/reme)

> First template with NO upstream image (ReMe ships only a pip package) and NO upstream authentication - upstream states the service layer has none, CORS allows any origin, jobs may write/move/delete files, and instructs users to place it behind an authenticated reverse proxy rather than exposing it directly. So Caddy holds a generated bcrypt credential as the only public listener while ReMe keeps its default 127.0.0.1 bind and service.host=0.0.0.0 is never set. All 142 resolved packages are hash-pinned in requirements.lock because without an upstream image a rebuild is a fresh resolution; regenerate on linux/amd64. reme-ai declares reme_studio with no version constraint, so the lock is what stops the UI drifting. Apache-2.0. See guide §5.32.

### Scrumboy — 🔴 update recommended

Template repo [RockinPaul/scrumboy_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/scrumboy_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-18) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/scrumboy-1)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image plus curl and tini. The entrypoint claims the Owner account against a loopback-only listener before the public port opens, because Scrumboy gives ownership to the first caller of /api/auth/bootstrap and serves anonymous reads and writes until then. Image tags are semver WITHOUT a v prefix; the git tag has one. AGPL-3.0. See guide \u00a75.29.

  > `mise bootstrap` can now manage MacPorts ports and update mise itself before tools. GitHub, GitLab and Forgejo tools can follow the most recently published release, and Homebrew tap formulae can pour bottles and download from private GitHub releases. This release also fixes `http:` checksum verification, shims for `path:` versions, `mise unuse -g` with split global configs, Windows Server Core startup, and several task, bootstrap and Java issues.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > ### Bootstrap
  > 
  > - **MacPorts package manager.** On macOS, you can declare ports in `[bootstrap.packages]` with the `macports:` prefix and i …
- [v2026.11.10](https://github.com/jdx/mise/releases/tag/v2026.11.10) 2026-11-18
  > `mise lock` gains an offline `--check` mode for CI, and `--dry-run` now shows what would actually change. New options let you limit mise to an allowlist of backends, choose musl or glibc builds for a single tool, pin PyPI packages to private uv indexes, and exclude git-ignored files from task `sources`. This release also fixes problems with lockfiles, Java build ordering, `mise exec` PATH order, Homebrew formulas, and shims.
  > 
  > ## Added
  > 
  > ### Lockfiles
  >

CHANGELOG lines added since the pin:
> ### Fixes
> - Notes with newline-separated preferences or facts now keep each claim in
>   stored summaries without splitting wrapped lines into separate statements.
> - Embedded project-scoped writes. `longmemory_ingest` with a `project_id`,
>   `longmemory_remember_decision` and connector imports go through
>   `applyImportPlan`, which stored every node without a semantic vector, so
>   those memories never received a vector score in recall (#216).
> - The HTTP server never read `LONGMEMORY_TENANT_ID`/`LONGMEMORY_USER_ID`, so
>   every restart silently re-scoped the engine (and the `/mcp` endpoint) to the
>   `default` tenant and user, making memories saved under a configured scope
>   unreachable after a restart (#215).
> - MCP tool responses (`longmemory_ingest`, `longmemory_recall`, and others)
>   embedded each node's full embedding vector, repeated across every place the
>   node is nested in a response, inflating responses by thousands of tokens.
>   Vectors are now stripped by default (`LONGMEMORY_MCP_INCLUDE_VECTORS=true`
>   or a per-call `include_vectors: true` input restores them); ingest and
>   recall responses also no longer repeat a node already returned in full
>   elsewhere in the same response, …

CHANGELOG lines added since the pin:
> ## [1.0.3b7] - 2026-11-17
> ### 新增
> - 托管专家可选会话模式，并能使用连接器、知识库、专家回调和已启用的插件
> - 云服务器页的地域筛选改为按大区分组的下拉面板
> ### 变更
> - 工具调用上限只拦截连续的相同调用
> - 云服务器页侧栏折叠、字号和未授权提示与对话页对齐
> ### 修复
> - Docker 部署时仍可将宿主机目录挂进 Docker 专家
> - Telegram 回复本机器人时保留引用；记忆列表不再被过期响应覆盖
> - PostgreSQL 清理孤儿 checkpoint 时只统计，不再误删其他智能体的对话
> - 带路径的 MCP OAuth 按规范发现授权信息
> - 窄屏下 Word / PDF 左侧可以滚到；新手引导遮罩铺满窗口
> ## [1.0.3b6] - 2026-11-15
> ### 新增
> - 支持 Helm 集群部署。多实例共用一个 PostgreSQL
> - 管理员可以限制创建专家时能选的存储后端
> - 新增托管专家，对话可以交给后端 Agent，如 OpenCode
> - 知识库支持知识图谱和重排序
> - 新增本地模型供应商 Magpie
> - 定时任务可以跟最新对话，也可以固定到某一场历史对话
> - 对话里列出本轮改过的文件、所在目录和增删行数
> ### 修复
> - 编辑托管专家时，不显示存储后端也能保存
> ### 安全
> - 不能再把宿主机目录挂进 Docker 专家
> - 腾讯云登录按用户隔离
> ## [1.0.3b5] - 2026-11-11
> ### 新增
> - 自定义 MCP 可以填写 OAuth 应用凭证（Client ID / Secret、Scope、授权与令牌端点）：授权服务器不支持自动注册客户端，或不提供发现信息时，也能在页面上完成授权并自动刷新令牌
> - 自定义 MCP 服务器的 OAuth 授权失效或尚未授权时，对话会提示"授权并重试"，在弹窗中授权后自动重发原消息；授权被取消或失败时会提示原因
> - 新增 `octop migrate openclaw` / `octop migrate hermes`：把原有助手的模型供应商、人格与记忆文件、技能、MCP 服务器和通道迁移到一个专家，支持 `--dry-run` 预演；已存在的内容不会被覆盖，通道默认不启用
> - 知识库的文档和文件夹可以移动到同一知识库的其他文件夹或根目录：卡片与表格的操作栏新增「移动到…」，文件夹会连同其中的内容一起移动，无需重新上传和索引
> - 知识库的文档和文件夹也可以移动到其他知识库：移动弹窗按知识库列出目标文件夹，原文件和索引一起迁移，无需重新上传
> - 知识库检索支持重排序（Rerank）：在「知识库设置」里选择提供重排序模型的供应商后，向量检索会先多取一批候选片段，再按与问题的相关度重排，只保留最相关的几条；重排序请求失败时自动沿用向量顺序
> - …

### QwenPaw — 🟠 review

Template repo [RockinPaul/qwenpaw_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/qwenpaw_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-18) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/qwenpaw-1)

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image, used unmodified - only env vars and an entrypoint. Requires a login upstream leaves optional, and deliberately runs NO relay, because QwenPaw skips auth for 127.0.0.1/::1 and an in-container proxy would silently disable the login (measured 200 via relay vs 401 direct). Rebinding to :: was tried and reverted, since Python sets IPV6_V6ONLY and Railway's public proxy is IPv4. Auto-registration variables are write-once, and so is the agent language: upstream defaults to zh with no env var and no init flag, and its non-interactive init copies persona files with skip_existing=True, so the wrapper seeds config.json and runs init itself before handing off (QWENPAW_AGENT_LANGUAGE, default en). Apache-2.0. See guide §5.31.

### PaddleOCR — 🟡 minor drift

Template repo [RockinPaul/paddleocr_railway_template](https://github.com/RockinPaul/paddleocr_railway_template) (last commit 2026-11-18) · [Railway listing](https://railway.com/deploy/paddleocr)

> Second template with no upstream image (PaddleOCR ships pip packages only) and no upstream authentication, so Caddy holds a generated credential and paddlex --serve is pinned to 127.0.0.1 rather than its 0.0.0.0 default. FIRST template with NO VOLUME: stateless, and the pipeline's 5 models (177 MB) are baked into the image because PaddleX downloads them when the SERVER starts, not lazily. The first inference then costs 98s versus ~4s warm, so the entrypoint runs a throwaway inference before the proxy opens. PADDLE_PDX_ENABLE_MKLDNN_BYDEFAULT=False is REQUIRED: oneDNN breaks every request on real x86 with PaddlePaddle 3.3.1, and a local emulated rehearsal cannot reproduce it. Apache-2.0. See guide §5.33.

> Thin wrapper over upstream's own multi-arch image, used unmodified. Adds Railway environment plus a guard that refuses to start without TRIVY_TOKEN, because an empty token disables authentication entirely and upstream's own guidance for an exposed server is to configure one. No browser surface; the listing leads with the client command. Image and git tags are both plain semver with a v prefix on the git side. Apache-2.0. See guide \u00a75.30.
